# November 05 2026 Now Assist Suite release notes

Now Assist Suite version and compatibility information for the November 05 2026 release on the ServiceNow Store.

Note: Now Assist Suite versions are cumulative. For app version updates that have been carried forward from previous releases, refer to the release notes page for the application's "Last updated" date or the ServiceNow Store app listing. See [Now Assist suite versions in the Application Manager](https://www.servicenow.com/docs/r/platform-administration/application-manager/now-assist-suites-app-mgr.html) for more details.

## Suite version 28.12.20261105 - Zurich Patch 12

<table><thead><tr><th>

App name

</th><th>

Version number

</th><th>

Release notes

</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>

AI Agents for Service Exchange Provider

</td><td>

1.1.13

</td><td>

Fixed

-   Fixed the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant replying "Service Exchange is not installed on this instance" on instances where Service Exchange was installed through the provider or consumer tier. The assistant now detects the installed version and answers the question.
-   Fixed tables owned by another application being re-scoped to a different scope when the app was installed, which caused cross-scope issues.
-   Corrected the access control names for the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant and the Service Exchange Assistant so they follow the platform's standard naming.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Data Explorer

</td><td>

5.2.14

</td><td>

Fixed

Edit Sources modal improvements

-   Fixed an issue where the formula indicators breakdown selector did not show the 'is one of' operator.
-   Fixed an issue where View source showed the metric as Count when the chart was calculating an Average.
-   Fixed an issue where the Edit Sources filter showed a relative date window that was recalculated each time it was opened. The filter now matches the window used to generate the answer.

Add to exploration flow

-   Fixed an issue where Add to exploration did not add items when started from a classic list.
-   Fixed an issue where Add to exploration did not add items to an exploration that was open in a different browser tab.
-   Fixed an issue where AI Data Explorer skills appeared disabled in a child domain after the skill was edited there.
-   Fixed an issue where the chart resize handle was unresponsive on a newly rendered chart until the page was refreshed.
-   Fixed an issue where the Additional insights section displayed 'undefined' when insights could not be generated. The section is now hidden in that case.

</td></tr><tr><td>

Conversation Improvement themes

</td><td>

1.0.8

</td><td>

Fixed a bug that was causing the transcript of a conversation not to build correctly for primary request tagging.

</td></tr><tr><td>

Intelligent Approvals

</td><td>

2.4.2

</td><td>

New

Added support for the latest model \(3.5\) of Google Gemini.

</td></tr><tr><td>

IT Service Management AI agent collection

</td><td>

12.1.0

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 for Engagement skills \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. \* The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.Removed\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM Autonomous Workforce

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

-   Service Desk Managers get a Rate trend chart on the AI Specialist Performance dashboard plotting the durable auto-resolve and coverage rates over time, with each day's incidents a click away.
-   Service Desk Managers get a four-step funnel on the Overview tab, from incidents assigned to eligible groups to incidents durably resolved, with drill-down and cards explaining each rate.
-   Service Desk Managers get a chart of what the service desk did with cross-group reassignment recommendations: adopted, overridden, or awaiting a decision.
-   AI Admins can set the portal that knowledge and catalog links open for requesters, while Service Desk Agents keep platform links in work notes.
-   Requesters see the catalog item variables the AI Specialist submitted in the Solution Proposed comment.

Changed

-   Requesters are asked fewer catalog questions, because the AI Specialist fills variables from the incident and the caller's details and shows what it already captured.
-   Requesters receive catalog follow-up questions composed from instruction-mode templates, with the fixed template as a fallback.
-   Service Desk Agents see a knowledge article in Attached Knowledge only once the incident it resolved is closed.
-   Service Desk Managers can apply the Assignment Group and AI Specialist filters together on the All incident outcomes chart.
-   Service Desk Managers on non-English instances see the Catalog tab, funnel, cross-group outcomes, and catalog indicators in their own language, in up to 24 locales.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.
-   Cited knowledge articles were dropped from requester comments by a faulty access check, and were missing from work notes after a low-confidence fallback or reassignment.
-   Saving activity notes failed when research included an uncited result from a table the AI Specialist cannot read.
-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.
-   Resolution notes were cut off at the Helpful References section when none of its links remained.
-   A knowledge feedback task was created for incidents resolved with a catalog item.
-   Incidents stayed In Progress when the Solution proposed state was mapped to Resolved or Closed.
-   A requester comment arriving just after work started could start a duplicate run on the same incident.
-   The closing note after a requester confirmed the fix was always written in English.
-   Canadian French was treated as French.
-   Notes could post with template slots unfilled. The AI Specialist now retries and hands off to a person after three failures.
-   The AI Specialist could ignore the operating system of the computer linked to the incident.
-   Reloading a dashboard drilldown page failed, and unused article links opened a not found page.
-   After an upgrade to version 1.2.2, the dashboard could be missing earlier improvements and show identical first two routing journey metrics.
-   Attachment summarization did not work out of the box, because its service URL property shipped empty.
-   A deprecated AI agent could run instead of the AI Specialist agent after an upgrade.

Removed

-   The Routing journey count cards are gone from the Detailed view, replaced by the Overview funnel.
-   The unused Research depth setting is gone.
-   The Pre resolution condition setting is gone, and incidents are no longer checked against it.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM L1 AI Specialist

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

-   Service Desk Managers get a Rate trend chart on the AI Specialist Performance dashboard plotting the durable auto-resolve and coverage rates over time, with each day's incidents a click away.
-   Service Desk Managers get a four-step funnel on the Overview tab, from incidents assigned to eligible groups to incidents durably resolved, with drill-down and cards explaining each rate.
-   Service Desk Managers get a chart of what the service desk did with cross-group reassignment recommendations: adopted, overridden, or awaiting a decision.
-   AI Admins can set the portal that knowledge and catalog links open for requesters, while Service Desk Agents keep platform links in work notes.
-   Requesters see the catalog item variables the AI Specialist submitted in the Solution Proposed comment.

Changed

-   Requesters are asked fewer catalog questions, because the AI Specialist fills variables from the incident and the caller's details and shows what it already captured.
-   Requesters receive catalog follow-up questions composed from instruction-mode templates, with the fixed template as a fallback.
-   Service Desk Agents see a knowledge article in Attached Knowledge only once the incident it resolved is closed.
-   Service Desk Managers can apply the Assignment Group and AI Specialist filters together on the All incident outcomes chart.
-   Service Desk Managers on non-English instances see the Catalog tab, funnel, cross-group outcomes, and catalog indicators in their own language, in up to 24 locales.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.
-   Cited knowledge articles were dropped from requester comments by a faulty access check, and were missing from work notes after a low-confidence fallback or reassignment.
-   Saving activity notes failed when research included an uncited result from a table the AI Specialist cannot read.
-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.
-   Resolution notes were cut off at the Helpful References section when none of its links remained.
-   A knowledge feedback task was created for incidents resolved with a catalog item.
-   Incidents stayed In Progress when the Solution proposed state was mapped to Resolved or Closed.
-   A requester comment arriving just after work started could start a duplicate run on the same incident.
-   The closing note after a requester confirmed the fix was always written in English.
-   Canadian French was treated as French.
-   Notes could post with template slots unfilled. The AI Specialist now retries and hands off to a person after three failures.
-   The AI Specialist could ignore the operating system of the computer linked to the incident.
-   Reloading a dashboard drilldown page failed, and unused article links opened a not found page.
-   After an upgrade to version 1.2.2, the dashboard could be missing earlier improvements and show identical first two routing journey metrics.
-   Attachment summarization did not work out of the box, because its service URL property shipped empty.
-   A deprecated AI agent could run instead of the AI Specialist agent after an upgrade.

Removed

-   The Routing journey count cards are gone from the Detailed view, replaced by the Overview funnel.
-   The unused Research depth setting is gone.
-   The Pre resolution condition setting is gone, and incidents are no longer checked against it.

</td></tr><tr><td>

Microsoft Azure AI Speech Spoke

</td><td>

1.0.1

</td><td>

Fixed: ACLs for voice input being sent to NowLLM.

</td></tr><tr><td>

Now Assist for Platform for Requestor

</td><td>

3.1.0

</td><td>

Updated release dependencies.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for Digital End-user Experience \(DEX\)

</td><td>

5.3.0

</td><td>

Fixed

The DEX remediation trigger AI agent now receives the device operating system. Supported remedial actions run on end-user devices instead of being blocked.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for IT Service Management \(ITSM\)

</td><td>

18.1.0

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 model updates \* Incident summarization \(New, In progress, Resolved, and Closed\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Record resolution \(resolution notes generation\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Request, requested item, and catalog task summarization now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* Change summarization and change risk explanation now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* The Change risk assessment Assessment Answers Generator Gemini configuration now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash instead of Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. \* Release notes generation now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous configurations are kept but are no longer the default. \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.\* Resolve modal in Service Operations Workspace \* Hyperlinks pasted into Additional comments when resolving an incident are now kept as clickable links.\* AI Search Assist for incident record producers \* AI Search Assist now returns a no-response result when it produces no answer, so the AI Search fallback results are used. \* It now uses the search profile configured for the record producer when one is set.\* Translation fixes addedRemoved\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for Service Exchange

</td><td>

1.1.13

</td><td>

Fixed

-   Fixed the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant replying "Service Exchange is not installed on this instance" on instances where Service Exchange was installed through the provider or consumer tier. The assistant now detects the installed version and answers the question.
-   Fixed tables owned by another application being re-scoped to a different scope when the app was installed, which caused cross-scope issues.
-   Corrected the access control names for the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant and the Service Exchange Assistant so they follow the platform's standard naming.

</td></tr><tr><td>

1.1.0

AI Agents for Service Exchange Provider

</td><td>

1.1.13

</td><td>

Fixed

-   Fixed the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant replying "Service Exchange is not installed on this instance" on instances where Service Exchange was installed through the provider or consumer tier. The assistant now detects the installed version and answers the question.
-   Fixed tables owned by another application being re-scoped to a different scope when the app was installed, which caused cross-scope issues.
-   Corrected the access control names for the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant and the Service Exchange Assistant so they follow the platform's standard naming.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Data Explorer

</td><td>

5.2.14

</td><td>

Fixed

Edit Sources modal improvements

-   Fixed an issue where the formula indicators breakdown selector did not show the 'is one of' operator.
-   Fixed an issue where View source showed the metric as Count when the chart was calculating an Average.
-   Fixed an issue where the Edit Sources filter showed a relative date window that was recalculated each time it was opened. The filter now matches the window used to generate the answer.

Add to exploration flow

-   Fixed an issue where Add to exploration did not add items when started from a classic list.
-   Fixed an issue where Add to exploration did not add items to an exploration that was open in a different browser tab.
-   Fixed an issue where AI Data Explorer skills appeared disabled in a child domain after the skill was edited there.
-   Fixed an issue where the chart resize handle was unresponsive on a newly rendered chart until the page was refreshed.
-   Fixed an issue where the Additional insights section displayed 'undefined' when insights could not be generated. The section is now hidden in that case.

</td></tr><tr><td>

</td><td>

2.1.7

</td><td>

This release contains minor defect fixes.

</td></tr><tr><td>

Conversation Improvement themes

</td><td>

1.0.8

</td><td>

Fixed a bug that was causing the transcript of a conversation not to build correctly for primary request tagging.

12.1.0

Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.Removed\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM Autonomous Workforce

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

-   Service Desk Managers get a Rate trend chart on the AI Specialist Performance dashboard plotting the durable auto-resolve and coverage rates over time, with each day's incidents a click away.
-   Service Desk Managers get a four-step funnel on the Overview tab, from incidents assigned to eligible groups to incidents durably resolved, with drill-down and cards explaining each rate.
-   Service Desk Managers get a chart of what the service desk did with cross-group reassignment recommendations: adopted, overridden, or awaiting a decision.
-   AI Admins can set the portal that knowledge and catalog links open for requesters, while Service Desk Agents keep platform links in work notes.
-   Requesters see the catalog item variables the AI Specialist submitted in the Solution Proposed comment.

Changed

-   Requesters are asked fewer catalog questions, because the AI Specialist fills variables from the incident and the caller's details and shows what it already captured.
-   Requesters receive catalog follow-up questions composed from instruction-mode templates, with the fixed template as a fallback.
-   Service Desk Agents see a knowledge article in Attached Knowledge only once the incident it resolved is closed.
-   Service Desk Managers can apply the Assignment Group and AI Specialist filters together on the All incident outcomes chart.
-   Service Desk Managers on non-English instances see the Catalog tab, funnel, cross-group outcomes, and catalog indicators in their own language, in up to 24 locales.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.
-   Cited knowledge articles were dropped from requester comments by a faulty access check, and were missing from work notes after a low-confidence fallback or reassignment.
-   Saving activity notes failed when research included an uncited result from a table the AI Specialist cannot read.
-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.
-   Resolution notes were cut off at the Helpful References section when none of its links remained.
-   A knowledge feedback task was created for incidents resolved with a catalog item.
-   Incidents stayed In Progress when the Solution proposed state was mapped to Resolved or Closed.
-   A requester comment arriving just after work started could start a duplicate run on the same incident.
-   The closing note after a requester confirmed the fix was always written in English.
-   Canadian French was treated as French.
-   Notes could post with template slots unfilled. The AI Specialist now retries and hands off to a person after three failures.
-   The AI Specialist could ignore the operating system of the computer linked to the incident.
-   Reloading a dashboard drilldown page failed, and unused article links opened a not found page.
-   After an upgrade to version 1.2.2, the dashboard could be missing earlier improvements and show identical first two routing journey metrics.
-   Attachment summarization did not work out of the box, because its service URL property shipped empty.
-   A deprecated AI agent could run instead of the AI Specialist agent after an upgrade.

Removed

-   The Routing journey count cards are gone from the Detailed view, replaced by the Overview funnel.
-   The unused Research depth setting is gone.
-   The Pre resolution condition setting is gone, and incidents are no longer checked against it.

</td></tr><tr><td>

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

ServiceNow Otto for IT Service Management \(ITSM\)

</td><td>

18.1.0

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 model updates \* Incident summarization \(New, In progress, Resolved, and Closed\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Record resolution \(resolution notes generation\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Request, requested item, and catalog task summarization now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* Change summarization and change risk explanation now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* The Change risk assessment Assessment Answers Generator Gemini configuration now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash instead of Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. \* Release notes generation now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous configurations are kept but are no longer the default. \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.\* Resolve modal in Service Operations Workspace \* Hyperlinks pasted into Additional comments when resolving an incident are now kept as clickable links.\* AI Search Assist for incident record producers \* AI Search Assist now returns a no-response result when it produces no answer, so the AI Search fallback results are used. \* It now uses the search profile configured for the record producer when one is set.\* Translation fixes addedRemoved\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for Service Exchange

</td><td>

1.1.13

</td><td>

Fixed

-   Fixed the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant replying "Service Exchange is not installed on this instance" on instances where Service Exchange was installed through the provider or consumer tier. The assistant now detects the installed version and answers the question.
-   Fixed tables owned by another application being re-scoped to a different scope when the app was installed, which caused cross-scope issues.
-   Corrected the access control names for the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant and the Service Exchange Assistant so they follow the platform's standard naming.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for Setup

</td><td>

6.1.1

</td><td>

New

Premium chat is the enhanced Otto chat experience that coordinates AI agents and skills behind the scenes to complete multi-step tasks.

-   ServiceNow Otto for Setup now runs exclusively on Premium chat. Hierarchical Agents have been disabled, along with the related Hierarchical Agents infrastructure and conversion artifacts.
-   ServiceNow Otto for Setup requires Premium chat. Customers who don't have Premium chat enabled see a banner prompting them to turn it on, with a Go to settings button that links directly to the settings page.

## Suite version 29.5.20261105 - Australia Patch 5

<table><thead><tr><th>

App name

</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>

AI Agents for Service Exchange Provider

</td><td>

1.1.13

</td><td>

Fixed

-   Fixed the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant replying "Service Exchange is not installed on this instance" on instances where Service Exchange was installed through the provider or consumer tier. The assistant now detects the installed version and answers the question.
-   Fixed tables owned by another application being re-scoped to a different scope when the app was installed, which caused cross-scope issues.
-   Corrected the access control names for the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant and the Service Exchange Assistant so they follow the platform's standard naming.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Data Explorer

</td><td>

5.2.14

</td><td>

Fixed

Edit Sources modal improvements

-   Fixed an issue where the formula indicators breakdown selector did not show the 'is one of' operator.
-   Fixed an issue where View source showed the metric as Count when the chart was calculating an Average.
-   Fixed an issue where the Edit Sources filter showed a relative date window that was recalculated each time it was opened. The filter now matches the window used to generate the answer.

Add to exploration flow

-   Fixed an issue where Add to exploration did not add items when started from a classic list.
-   Fixed an issue where Add to exploration did not add items to an exploration that was open in a different browser tab.
-   Fixed an issue where AI Data Explorer skills appeared disabled in a child domain after the skill was edited there.
-   Fixed an issue where the chart resize handle was unresponsive on a newly rendered chart until the page was refreshed.
-   Fixed an issue where the Additional insights section displayed 'undefined' when insights could not be generated. The section is now hidden in that case.

</td></tr><tr><td>

Conversation Improvement themes

</td><td>

1.0.8

</td><td>

Fixed a bug that was causing the transcript of a conversation not to build correctly for primary request tagging.

</td></tr><tr><td>

IT Service Management AI agent collection

12.1.0

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 for Engagement skills \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. \* The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.Removed\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM Autonomous Workforce

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

-   Service Desk Managers get a Rate trend chart on the AI Specialist Performance dashboard plotting the durable auto-resolve and coverage rates over time, with each day's incidents a click away.
-   Service Desk Managers get a four-step funnel on the Overview tab, from incidents assigned to eligible groups to incidents durably resolved, with drill-down and cards explaining each rate.
-   Service Desk Managers get a chart of what the service desk did with cross-group reassignment recommendations: adopted, overridden, or awaiting a decision.
-   AI Admins can set the portal that knowledge and catalog links open for requesters, while Service Desk Agents keep platform links in work notes.
-   Requesters see the catalog item variables the AI Specialist submitted in the Solution Proposed comment.

Changed

-   Requesters are asked fewer catalog questions, because the AI Specialist fills variables from the incident and the caller's details and shows what it already captured.
-   Requesters receive catalog follow-up questions composed from instruction-mode templates, with the fixed template as a fallback.
-   Service Desk Agents see a knowledge article in Attached Knowledge only once the incident it resolved is closed.
-   Service Desk Managers can apply the Assignment Group and AI Specialist filters together on the All incident outcomes chart.
-   Service Desk Managers on non-English instances see the Catalog tab, funnel, cross-group outcomes, and catalog indicators in their own language, in up to 24 locales.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.
-   Cited knowledge articles were dropped from requester comments by a faulty access check, and were missing from work notes after a low-confidence fallback or reassignment.
-   Saving activity notes failed when research included an uncited result from a table the AI Specialist cannot read.
-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.
-   Resolution notes were cut off at the Helpful References section when none of its links remained.
-   A knowledge feedback task was created for incidents resolved with a catalog item.
-   Incidents stayed In Progress when the Solution proposed state was mapped to Resolved or Closed.
-   A requester comment arriving just after work started could start a duplicate run on the same incident.
-   The closing note after a requester confirmed the fix was always written in English.
-   Canadian French was treated as French.
-   Notes could post with template slots unfilled. The AI Specialist now retries and hands off to a person after three failures.
-   The AI Specialist could ignore the operating system of the computer linked to the incident.
-   Reloading a dashboard drilldown page failed, and unused article links opened a not found page.
-   After an upgrade to version 1.2.2, the dashboard could be missing earlier improvements and show identical first two routing journey metrics.
-   Attachment summarization did not work out of the box, because its service URL property shipped empty.
-   A deprecated AI agent could run instead of the AI Specialist agent after an upgrade.

Removed

-   The Routing journey count cards are gone from the Detailed view, replaced by the Overview funnel.
-   The unused Research depth setting is gone.
-   The Pre resolution condition setting is gone, and incidents are no longer checked against it.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM L1 AI Specialist

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

Fixed

-   Fixed the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant replying "Service Exchange is not installed on this instance" on instances where Service Exchange was installed through the provider or consumer tier. The assistant now detects the installed version and answers the question.
-   Fixed tables owned by another application being re-scoped to a different scope when the app was installed, which caused cross-scope issues.
-   Corrected the access control names for the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant and the Service Exchange Assistant so they follow the platform's standard naming.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for Digital End-user Experience \(DEX\)

</td><td>

5.3.0

</td><td>

Fixed

The DEX remediation trigger AI agent now receives the device operating system. Supported remedial actions run on end-user devices instead of being blocked.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for IT Service Management \(ITSM\)

</td><td>

18.1.0

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 model updates \* Incident summarization \(New, In progress, Resolved, and Closed\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Record resolution \(resolution notes generation\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Request, requested item, and catalog task summarization now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* Change summarization and change risk explanation now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* The Change risk assessment Assessment Answers Generator Gemini configuration now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash instead of Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. \* Release notes generation now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous configurations are kept but are no longer the default. \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.\* Resolve modal in Service Operations Workspace \* Hyperlinks pasted into Additional comments when resolving an incident are now kept as clickable links.\* AI Search Assist for incident record producers \* AI Search Assist now returns a no-response result when it produces no answer, so the AI Search fallback results are used. \* It now uses the search profile configured for the record producer when one is set.\* Translation fixes addedRemoved\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

1.1.13

-   Fixed the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant replying "Service Exchange is not installed on this instance" on instances where Service Exchange was installed through the provider or consumer tier. The assistant now detects the installed version and answers the question.
-   Fixed tables owned by another application being re-scoped to a different scope when the app was installed, which caused cross-scope issues.
-   Corrected the access control names for the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant and the Service Exchange Assistant so they follow the platform's standard naming.

## Suite version 29.6.20261105 - Australia Patch 6

AI Agents for Service Exchange Provider

</td><td>

1.1.13

</td><td>

Fixed

-   Fixed the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant replying "Service Exchange is not installed on this instance" on instances where Service Exchange was installed through the provider or consumer tier. The assistant now detects the installed version and answers the question.
-   Fixed tables owned by another application being re-scoped to a different scope when the app was installed, which caused cross-scope issues.
-   Corrected the access control names for the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant and the Service Exchange Assistant so they follow the platform's standard naming.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Data Explorer

</td><td>

5.2.14

</td><td>

Fixed

Edit Sources modal improvements

-   Fixed an issue where the formula indicators breakdown selector did not show the 'is one of' operator.
-   Fixed an issue where View source showed the metric as Count when the chart was calculating an Average.
-   Fixed an issue where the Edit Sources filter showed a relative date window that was recalculated each time it was opened. The filter now matches the window used to generate the answer.

Add to exploration flow

-   Fixed an issue where Add to exploration did not add items when started from a classic list.
-   Fixed an issue where Add to exploration did not add items to an exploration that was open in a different browser tab.
-   Fixed an issue where AI Data Explorer skills appeared disabled in a child domain after the skill was edited there.
-   Fixed an issue where the chart resize handle was unresponsive on a newly rendered chart until the page was refreshed.
-   Fixed an issue where the Additional insights section displayed 'undefined' when insights could not be generated. The section is now hidden in that case.

</td></tr><tr><td>

Backend defect fixes, AI agents' prompts refinement, and other minor enhancements.

12.1.0

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 for Engagement skills \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. \* The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.Removed\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM Autonomous Workforce

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

-   Service Desk Managers get a Rate trend chart on the AI Specialist Performance dashboard plotting the durable auto-resolve and coverage rates over time, with each day's incidents a click away.
-   Service Desk Managers get a four-step funnel on the Overview tab, from incidents assigned to eligible groups to incidents durably resolved, with drill-down and cards explaining each rate.
-   Service Desk Managers get a chart of what the service desk did with cross-group reassignment recommendations: adopted, overridden, or awaiting a decision.
-   AI Admins can set the portal that knowledge and catalog links open for requesters, while Service Desk Agents keep platform links in work notes.
-   Requesters see the catalog item variables the AI Specialist submitted in the Solution Proposed comment.

Changed

-   Requesters are asked fewer catalog questions, because the AI Specialist fills variables from the incident and the caller's details and shows what it already captured.
-   Requesters receive catalog follow-up questions composed from instruction-mode templates, with the fixed template as a fallback.
-   Service Desk Agents see a knowledge article in Attached Knowledge only once the incident it resolved is closed.
-   Service Desk Managers can apply the Assignment Group and AI Specialist filters together on the All incident outcomes chart.
-   Service Desk Managers on non-English instances see the Catalog tab, funnel, cross-group outcomes, and catalog indicators in their own language, in up to 24 locales.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.
-   Cited knowledge articles were dropped from requester comments by a faulty access check, and were missing from work notes after a low-confidence fallback or reassignment.
-   Saving activity notes failed when research included an uncited result from a table the AI Specialist cannot read.
-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.
-   Resolution notes were cut off at the Helpful References section when none of its links remained.
-   A knowledge feedback task was created for incidents resolved with a catalog item.
-   Incidents stayed In Progress when the Solution proposed state was mapped to Resolved or Closed.
-   A requester comment arriving just after work started could start a duplicate run on the same incident.
-   The closing note after a requester confirmed the fix was always written in English.
-   Canadian French was treated as French.
-   Notes could post with template slots unfilled. The AI Specialist now retries and hands off to a person after three failures.
-   The AI Specialist could ignore the operating system of the computer linked to the incident.
-   Reloading a dashboard drilldown page failed, and unused article links opened a not found page.
-   After an upgrade to version 1.2.2, the dashboard could be missing earlier improvements and show identical first two routing journey metrics.
-   Attachment summarization did not work out of the box, because its service URL property shipped empty.
-   A deprecated AI agent could run instead of the AI Specialist agent after an upgrade.

Removed

-   The Routing journey count cards are gone from the Detailed view, replaced by the Overview funnel.
-   The unused Research depth setting is gone.
-   The Pre resolution condition setting is gone, and incidents are no longer checked against it.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM L1 AI Specialist

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

-   Service Desk Managers get a Rate trend chart on the AI Specialist Performance dashboard plotting the durable auto-resolve and coverage rates over time, with each day's incidents a click away.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.

-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.

Fixed

-   Fixed the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant replying "Service Exchange is not installed on this instance" on instances where Service Exchange was installed through the provider or consumer tier. The assistant now detects the installed version and answers the question.
-   Fixed tables owned by another application being re-scoped to a different scope when the app was installed, which caused cross-scope issues.
-   Corrected the access control names for the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant and the Service Exchange Assistant so they follow the platform's standard naming.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for Digital End-user Experience \(DEX\)

</td><td>

5.3.0

</td><td>

Fixed

The DEX remediation trigger AI agent now receives the device operating system. Supported remedial actions run on end-user devices instead of being blocked.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for IT Service Management \(ITSM\)

</td><td>

18.1.0

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 model updates \* Incident summarization \(New, In progress, Resolved, and Closed\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Record resolution \(resolution notes generation\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Request, requested item, and catalog task summarization now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* Change summarization and change risk explanation now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* The Change risk assessment Assessment Answers Generator Gemini configuration now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash instead of Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. \* Release notes generation now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous configurations are kept but are no longer the default. \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.\* Resolve modal in Service Operations Workspace \* Hyperlinks pasted into Additional comments when resolving an incident are now kept as clickable links.\* AI Search Assist for incident record producers \* AI Search Assist now returns a no-response result when it produces no answer, so the AI Search fallback results are used. \* It now uses the search profile configured for the record producer when one is set.\* Translation fixes addedRemoved\* None.

-   Corrected the access control names for the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant and the Service Exchange Assistant so they follow the platform's standard naming.

## Suite version 29.7.20261105 - Australia Patch 7

<table><thead><tr><th>

App name

</th><th>

</th><th>

AI Agents for Enterprise

</td><td>

1.1.1

</td><td>

Generic LLM model prefixes have been replaced with specific model names for improved clarity and performance: Gemini Flash 3.5 \(formerly gemini\_small\), Claude Haiku 4.5 \(formerly claude\_small\), and GPT 5.4 mini \(formerly gpt\_small\).

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Agents for Service Exchange Provider

</td><td>

1.1.13

</td><td>

Fixed

-   Fixed the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant replying "Service Exchange is not installed on this instance" on instances where Service Exchange was installed through the provider or consumer tier. The assistant now detects the installed version and answers the question.
-   Fixed tables owned by another application being re-scoped to a different scope when the app was installed, which caused cross-scope issues.
-   Corrected the access control names for the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant and the Service Exchange Assistant so they follow the platform's standard naming.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Data Explorer

</td><td>

5.2.14

</td><td>

Fixed

Edit Sources modal improvements

-   Fixed an issue where the formula indicators breakdown selector did not show the 'is one of' operator.
-   Fixed an issue where View source showed the metric as Count when the chart was calculating an Average.
-   Fixed an issue where the Edit Sources filter showed a relative date window that was recalculated each time it was opened. The filter now matches the window used to generate the answer.

Add to exploration flow

-   Fixed an issue where Add to exploration did not add items when started from a classic list.
-   Fixed an issue where Add to exploration did not add items to an exploration that was open in a different browser tab.
-   Fixed an issue where AI Data Explorer skills appeared disabled in a child domain after the skill was edited there.
-   Fixed an issue where the chart resize handle was unresponsive on a newly rendered chart until the page was refreshed.
-   Fixed an issue where the Additional insights section displayed 'undefined' when insights could not be generated. The section is now hidden in that case.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Specialists for HR Service Delivery

</td><td>

1.1.1

</td><td>

Fixed

Backend defect fixes, AI agents' prompts refinement, and other minor enhancements.

</td></tr><tr><td>

Assist Order Management AI Agent

</td><td>

2.1.7

</td><td>

This release contains minor defect fixes.

</td></tr><tr><td>

</td><td>

2.1.0

NewThe Get CI Topology and Get Impacted Items tools use the V2 Impact Analysis agent.

ChangedGet CI Topology takes a change request or incident number in the task\_number parameter instead of a CI sys\_id in ci\_sys\_id. Update MCP clients to pass task\_number. Get Impacted Items continues to take change\_request\_number.

Field Service Management AI agent collection

</td><td>

4.2.1

</td><td>

New

-   FSM AI Native features now support GEMA model as ServiceNow-hosted option. All FSM AI skills and agents have been updated to support the GEMA model, replacing deprecated April and GPT OSS models. Guardian moderation integrations have been validated for compatibility.
-   FSM AI feature gating now supports mixed-SKU customer entitlements. Customers with Pro Plus or Enterprise Plus for FSM retain access to Prime-level AI features even if they hold lower-tier SKUs for other products. Subscription records have been updated to reflect correct product family mappings.

Changed

-   FSM AI subscription records updated for Pro Plus / Foundation mixed-SKU mapping. Product Family fields have been populated on all relevant subscription records, ensuring accurate entitlement resolution for mixed-SKU customers.
-   FSM AI Native skills migrated from deprecated April and GPT OSS models to GEMA. All skills previously targeting April or GPT OSS now default to GEMA or other supported models, ensuring continued functionality.

Removed

-   Sidebar Summarization \(FSM\) asset subscription record has been removed. The asset subscription record for Sidebar Summarization is no longer available, and associated variable sets are not gated behind FSM Advanced.
-   Now LLM LTS Generic provider configs for Work Order Task Summarization have been deprecated. All dormant configurations referencing deprecated LTS models have been deactivated, with no functional gap introduced.

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 for Engagement skills \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. \* The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.Removed\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM Autonomous Workforce

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

-   Service Desk Managers get a Rate trend chart on the AI Specialist Performance dashboard plotting the durable auto-resolve and coverage rates over time, with each day's incidents a click away.
-   Service Desk Managers get a four-step funnel on the Overview tab, from incidents assigned to eligible groups to incidents durably resolved, with drill-down and cards explaining each rate.
-   Service Desk Managers get a chart of what the service desk did with cross-group reassignment recommendations: adopted, overridden, or awaiting a decision.
-   AI Admins can set the portal that knowledge and catalog links open for requesters, while Service Desk Agents keep platform links in work notes.
-   Requesters see the catalog item variables the AI Specialist submitted in the Solution Proposed comment.

Changed

-   Requesters are asked fewer catalog questions, because the AI Specialist fills variables from the incident and the caller's details and shows what it already captured.
-   Requesters receive catalog follow-up questions composed from instruction-mode templates, with the fixed template as a fallback.
-   Service Desk Agents see a knowledge article in Attached Knowledge only once the incident it resolved is closed.
-   Service Desk Managers can apply the Assignment Group and AI Specialist filters together on the All incident outcomes chart.
-   Service Desk Managers on non-English instances see the Catalog tab, funnel, cross-group outcomes, and catalog indicators in their own language, in up to 24 locales.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.
-   Cited knowledge articles were dropped from requester comments by a faulty access check, and were missing from work notes after a low-confidence fallback or reassignment.
-   Saving activity notes failed when research included an uncited result from a table the AI Specialist cannot read.
-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.
-   Resolution notes were cut off at the Helpful References section when none of its links remained.
-   A knowledge feedback task was created for incidents resolved with a catalog item.
-   Incidents stayed In Progress when the Solution proposed state was mapped to Resolved or Closed.
-   A requester comment arriving just after work started could start a duplicate run on the same incident.
-   The closing note after a requester confirmed the fix was always written in English.
-   Canadian French was treated as French.
-   Notes could post with template slots unfilled. The AI Specialist now retries and hands off to a person after three failures.
-   The AI Specialist could ignore the operating system of the computer linked to the incident.
-   Reloading a dashboard drilldown page failed, and unused article links opened a not found page.
-   After an upgrade to version 1.2.2, the dashboard could be missing earlier improvements and show identical first two routing journey metrics.
-   Attachment summarization did not work out of the box, because its service URL property shipped empty.
-   A deprecated AI agent could run instead of the AI Specialist agent after an upgrade.

Removed

-   The Routing journey count cards are gone from the Detailed view, replaced by the Overview funnel.
-   The unused Research depth setting is gone.
-   The Pre resolution condition setting is gone, and incidents are no longer checked against it.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM L1 AI Specialist

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

-   Service Desk Managers get a Rate trend chart on the AI Specialist Performance dashboard plotting the durable auto-resolve and coverage rates over time, with each day's incidents a click away.
-   Service Desk Managers get a four-step funnel on the Overview tab, from incidents assigned to eligible groups to incidents durably resolved, with drill-down and cards explaining each rate.
-   Service Desk Managers get a chart of what the service desk did with cross-group reassignment recommendations: adopted, overridden, or awaiting a decision.
-   AI Admins can set the portal that knowledge and catalog links open for requesters, while Service Desk Agents keep platform links in work notes.
-   Requesters see the catalog item variables the AI Specialist submitted in the Solution Proposed comment.

Changed

-   Requesters are asked fewer catalog questions, because the AI Specialist fills variables from the incident and the caller's details and shows what it already captured.
-   Requesters receive catalog follow-up questions composed from instruction-mode templates, with the fixed template as a fallback.
-   Service Desk Agents see a knowledge article in Attached Knowledge only once the incident it resolved is closed.
-   Service Desk Managers can apply the Assignment Group and AI Specialist filters together on the All incident outcomes chart.
-   Service Desk Managers on non-English instances see the Catalog tab, funnel, cross-group outcomes, and catalog indicators in their own language, in up to 24 locales.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.
-   Cited knowledge articles were dropped from requester comments by a faulty access check, and were missing from work notes after a low-confidence fallback or reassignment.
-   Saving activity notes failed when research included an uncited result from a table the AI Specialist cannot read.
-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.
-   Resolution notes were cut off at the Helpful References section when none of its links remained.
-   A knowledge feedback task was created for incidents resolved with a catalog item.
-   Incidents stayed In Progress when the Solution proposed state was mapped to Resolved or Closed.
-   A requester comment arriving just after work started could start a duplicate run on the same incident.
-   The closing note after a requester confirmed the fix was always written in English.
-   Canadian French was treated as French.
-   Notes could post with template slots unfilled. The AI Specialist now retries and hands off to a person after three failures.
-   The AI Specialist could ignore the operating system of the computer linked to the incident.
-   Reloading a dashboard drilldown page failed, and unused article links opened a not found page.
-   After an upgrade to version 1.2.2, the dashboard could be missing earlier improvements and show identical first two routing journey metrics.
-   Attachment summarization did not work out of the box, because its service URL property shipped empty.
-   A deprecated AI agent could run instead of the AI Specialist agent after an upgrade.

Removed

-   The Routing journey count cards are gone from the Detailed view, replaced by the Overview funnel.
-   The unused Research depth setting is gone.
-   The Pre resolution condition setting is gone, and incidents are no longer checked against it.

ServiceNow Otto for Field Service Management

</td><td>

11.2.1

</td><td>

New

-   FSM AI Native features now support GEMA model as ServiceNow-hosted option. All FSM AI skills and agents have been updated to support the GEMA model, replacing deprecated April and GPT OSS models. Guardian moderation integrations have been validated for compatibility.
-   FSM AI feature gating now supports mixed-SKU customer entitlements. Customers with Pro Plus or Enterprise Plus for FSM retain access to Prime-level AI features even if they hold lower-tier SKUs for other products. Subscription records have been updated to reflect correct product family mappings.

Changed

-   FSM AI subscription records updated for Pro Plus / Foundation mixed-SKU mapping. Product Family fields have been populated on all relevant subscription records, ensuring accurate entitlement resolution for mixed-SKU customers.
-   FSM AI Native skills migrated from deprecated April and GPT OSS models to GEMA. All skills previously targeting April or GPT OSS now default to GEMA or other supported models, ensuring continued functionality.

Removed

-   Sidebar Summarization \(FSM\) asset subscription record has been removed. The asset subscription record for Sidebar Summarization is no longer available, and associated variable sets are not gated behind FSM Advanced.
-   Now LLM LTS Generic provider configs for Work Order Task Summarization have been deprecated. All dormant configurations referencing deprecated LTS models have been deactivated, with no functional gap introduced.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for IT Service Management \(ITSM\)

</td><td>

18.1.0

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 model updates \* Incident summarization \(New, In progress, Resolved, and Closed\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Record resolution \(resolution notes generation\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Request, requested item, and catalog task summarization now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* Change summarization and change risk explanation now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* The Change risk assessment Assessment Answers Generator Gemini configuration now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash instead of Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. \* Release notes generation now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous configurations are kept but are no longer the default. \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.\* Resolve modal in Service Operations Workspace \* Hyperlinks pasted into Additional comments when resolving an incident are now kept as clickable links.\* AI Search Assist for incident record producers \* AI Search Assist now returns a no-response result when it produces no answer, so the AI Search fallback results are used. \* It now uses the search profile configured for the record producer when one is set.\* Translation fixes addedRemoved\* None.

@servicenow/sn-ai-engagement-experience

</td><td>

3.8.0

</td><td>

New

New LUX in-product experience components to support inline variant with improved visual designs.

Fixed

-   Resolved auto-refresh issues for sn-agentic-status-card.
-   Resolved issue with AI specialist test component showing an endlessly loading spinner.
-   Rendering issues with LUX components.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Admin Center

</td><td>

6.2.5

</td><td>

New features.

-   First release of AI Admin Center using the next generation Lux application experience and design system.
-   Instance health creates a consolidated view of AI plug-in installation and health tracking with integration back into Application Manager.
-   Upgrade planning analyzes your current instance against a targeted upgrade version allowing you to see what may be changed or impacted during the updgrade process. It covers areas such as new and deprecated features, impacts to customizations, and application compatibility changes all from one place.
-   Intent management enables creation and association of your AI agents to intents for better traceability, reporting, and case management.
-   System property registry provides an admin-facing view into AI related system properties, their descriptions, and current value. It enables transparency and better self-discovery of current config settings impacting AI on the instance.
-   Assist consumption analytics allows you to track and manage your AI usage. It supports views by asset type, usage bands, and user-attributed department.
-   Self-service troubleshooting conversational experience, including issue classification, diagnosis, and recommended remediation for core AI admin use cases.

Other.

-   Minor enhancements to existing capabilities related to AI Agent Advisor and settings.
-   Minor defect fixes.

AI Admin Hub

11.1.3

</td><td>

AI Agents for AIOps

</td><td>

1.16.1

Fixed

-   Fix REST endpoint ai\_insights/trigger when working in autonomous mode.
-   Cmn\_department record installed by AI Agents for AIOps plugin should be removed.
-   Avoid SRE execution when alert is closed by the AI Specialist.
-   Fix parsing issues in KB skill responses as JSON.
-   During manual assignment to ai worker, unneeded check for 'handle unassigned' is happening.
-   Workflow status marked as error in em\_gai and AI insights lost when autonomous summarization JSON has invalid escape sequences.
-   Run Service Analysis fails on alert\_id input causing 'No record found' error \(4% rate on customer instance\).

Changed

Support Gemma model for AI Search.

1.1.13

</td><td>

Fixed

-   Fixed the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant replying "Service Exchange is not installed on this instance" on instances where Service Exchange was installed through the provider or consumer tier. The assistant now detects the installed version and answers the question.
-   Fixed tables owned by another application being re-scoped to a different scope when the app was installed, which caused cross-scope issues.
-   Corrected the access control names for the Service Exchange Knowledge Assistant and the Service Exchange Assistant so they follow the platform's standard naming.
