</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Control Tower for Enterprise AI Foundation

</td><td>

1.9.7

</td><td>

Inventory and Discovery.

New

-   Shadow AI: Details on detected AI services, guidance for unknown users and devices, end-user input, an option to hide the UI, and a department block.
-   Connectors: Trace Collector discovers Tools. Hyperscaler connectors install automatically. Anthropic discovers Agents via Beta APIs and supports MID servers. GCP imports Gemini Enterprise App Agents with clearer errors. Microsoft adds attributes and tag-based discovery. Databricks adds Domain Separation. GitHub discovers organizations, repositories, and Copilot agents, with scheduled imports and Cloud and Enterprise support.

Changed

-   Shadow AI lifetime aggregations limited to 3 years.
-   GCP Beta API usage UX updated.

Fixed

-   Shadow AI UI performance and registry filtering.
-   AI Discovery Agentic Client support and staging domain fallback.
-   Anthropic false success on Test Connection; LangGraph duplicates and standalone run usage; several GCP issues.

Govern.

New

-   AI and Data Marketplace: Business users choose from approved use cases and models.
-   AI Policy Framework v1.2: MVP release with kill switch.

Fixed

-   Playbook and lifecycle fixes in AI Risk and Compliance, and AI Risk and Asset Management.
-   Rejection comments display; rejected requests set to "Cancelled."

Secure.

New

-   AI Security Operator role: Deactivate and reinstate agents without full AI Steward access.
-   AWH for AI Control Tower: Client-Based Access Control \(CBAC\).

Changed

-   Top recommendations ranks agents by critical security events.
-   Action required on upgrade: Base Traceloop Warehouse connection removed. Recreate it and its credential if you use Traceloop-based metrics. Sampling and max-skill-call defaults reset.
-   Security tab split into Overview, Runtime, Post-runtime, and Security insights.
-   Renames: "Correctness detection" and "Agent containment list."
-   Consent required for Post-runtime and Security insights settings.

Fixed

-   Four customer-reported bugs, including silent AWS Bedrock block/unblock failures.
-   Multiple fixes across anonymization, heatmaps, agent maps, Overview load time, and CBAC policy names.

Monitor.

New

-   Direct trace access for usage mismatches.
-   Severity-based grouping of score drops.
-   Agent activity chart filters by top or bottom five metrics.
-   Metric scores and LLM judge reasoning on session details.
-   Input and output toggle in trace details.

Changed

-   Arc traces evaluated at asset level for Agentic Client assets; Tools discovery and evaluation added.
-   Reduced insight noise; widget customization across AICT pages.

Fixed

-   Redundant session scores removed; Privileged Access Detector shows 0% in red.
-   Custom date selection and duplicate insights.

Measure.

Fixed

-   Functional and non-functional IT defects.
-   Creator metrics for managed and unmanaged AI systems.

Foundations.

Changed

AINPX UI: AI Gateway access management, runtime security configuration, a Security Insights tab, connector upgrades, and LLM-based deduplication.

Fixed

-   EA for AICT domain separation on AI Portfolio widgets.
-   AWH: correct Tool Groups count, MCP sync job errors, and catalog population on new instances.
-   Otto: manufacturer, vendor, and owned\_by details resolve correctly.

Full details: https://www.servicenow.com/docs/r/release-notes/ai-control-tower-rn.html.

</td></tr><tr><td>

7.1.2

AI Data Explorer

</td><td>

5.2.14

</td><td>

Fixed

Edit Sources modal improvements

-   Fixed an issue where the formula indicators breakdown selector did not show the 'is one of' operator.
-   Fixed an issue where View source showed the metric as Count when the chart was calculating an Average.
-   Fixed an issue where the Edit Sources filter showed a relative date window that was recalculated each time it was opened. The filter now matches the window used to generate the answer.

Add to exploration flow

-   Fixed an issue where Add to exploration did not add items when started from a classic list.
-   Fixed an issue where Add to exploration did not add items to an exploration that was open in a different browser tab.
-   Fixed an issue where AI Data Explorer skills appeared disabled in a child domain after the skill was edited there.
-   Fixed an issue where the chart resize handle was unresponsive on a newly rendered chart until the page was refreshed.
-   Fixed an issue where the Additional insights section displayed 'undefined' when insights could not be generated. The section is now hidden in that case.

AI Data Kit

</td><td>

9.1.6

</td><td>

Fixed

-   Analyst users no longer see the Save changes button on Active dataset records. Previously, selecting it refreshed the page and showed an error.
-   Text fields in the Edit Records window for datasets are now editable. Previously, all text fields were read-only.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Help Framework

</td><td>

2.0.7

</td><td>

-   Changed: Added fixedVariantInitialLineCount recommended property in AI Native Help backend.
-   Fixed: Field visibility and mandatory validation issues.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Native Experience for AI Control Tower - ServicenowAI

</td><td>

4.0.6

</td><td>

AI Control Tower adds new capabilities across discovery, governance, security, monitoring, and measurement of AI usage.

Inventory and Discovery

Shadow AI Discovery

New

-   Details about the AI service detected, including models used, users, departments, devices, and event data.
-   Guidance on unknown users/devices.
-   End-user input surfaced for AI services detected via ACC.
-   Ability to turn off this UI for data protection.
-   Block capability for departments using an AI service detected via ACC.

Changed

Lifetime aggregations are limited to 3 years.

Govern

New

AI and Data Marketplace : Streamline the request process for business users by allowing them to select from already approved use cases and models, reducing friction and complexity in submitting requests.

Secure

New

AI Security Operator role - lets teams delegate agent containment \(deactivate/reinstate\) without granting full AI Steward access.

Changed

-   Smarter Overview recommendations - "Top Recommendations" now ranks agents by critical security events \(with agent name, event count, threat categories\) instead of mixing in the raw events list; Overview and Post-runtime event lists are now consistent with each other.
-   Four customer-reported bugs Fixed: stale tabs after navigation, "View source record" misrouting to raw trace data, a hidden Security and Privacy tab in the legacy UI, and silent AWS Bedrock block/unblock failures.
-   Action required on upgrade: the base Traceloop Warehouse connection is Removed: customers using Traceloop-based external AI metrics must recreate the connection and credential; sampling-rate/max-skill-call defaults also reset \(existing Detection-enabled settings are untouched\).

Monitor

Changed

-   Arc dependency - Discover and Evaluate Arc traces at Asset Level for assets categorized as 'Agentic Client'.
-   Tools discovery and evaluation.
-   UI design enhancements for metric line graph and session details page.
-   Arc-Mosaic dependency to support InstanceInfoAPI.
-   TL insights enhancements \(Group insights to reduce the noise\).
-   Update EvalPurposeUsage\(Mismatch\) insight to redirect to trace-level view.
-   Add aggregated metric insight \(with categorize data\) with severity-level grouping.
-   Widget customization capability support across ALL AICT pages.

Measure

New

-   Sub-vendor AI costs: Set a rate for a specific service from an integrated vendor, such as an agent platform. The sub-vendor rate takes precedence over the vendor rate. The vendor rate applies to any usage that doesn't have a sub-vendor rate.
-   Subscription AI costs: Record per-seat subscription costs for integrated and non-integrated vendors. Specify the number of licensed seats, the cost per seat, and the contract dates. Optionally, add a one-time setup fee and distribute it over a set number of months so that it's included in the estimated monthly cost.

Changed

Assign costs to specific services from integrated vendors.

Include per-seat subscription costs in AI cost tracking.

For full details, see AI Control Tower release notes.

Known limitations

Customer Service Management AI agent collection

</td><td>

9.0.2

</td><td>

Similar historical case search: Fixed the similar historical case search for the CRM AI Specialist, so it finds and returns similar past cases correctly.

Duplicate detection: Enhanced duplicate detection for the CRM AI Specialist.

-   A confirmed duplicate now uses a single duplicate\_detected outcome across the worker state mapping, response template choices, CRM Case AI Agent instructions and the Post Activity Note action. The case closes and the run finishes instead of ending in Awaiting Info.
-   Duplicate detection now runs before sentiment in assess\_case. A duplicate skips sentiment, intent and field prediction. An escalation skips only field prediction.
-   Duplicates are handled in both execution modes. Autopilot posts the customer note and closes the case. Copilot keeps the case closed instead of routing it back to New and reassigning it.
-   The agent no longer waits for a customer reply after a duplicate note, so it doesn't run the follow-up step on a closed case.
-   Dashboard analytics record duplicate\_detected as the closed state.
-   The fetch\_activity\_note\_template and Save Activity Notes tools list the exact allowed states \(solution\_proposed, awaiting\_info, reassign, duplicate\_detected\). The agent now posts the correct customer note for a duplicate instead of the solution-proposed one.

Record lookup and Triage cases wrap-up

-   Records now resolve correctly when record numbers are duplicated across tables.
-   The Triage cases wrap-up can now update work notes by record number.

Triage cases flows: The "Interaction - Triage cases" trigger flows now run as the session user instead of the system user. Actions in these flows now respect the user's access and show the right user as the actor.

</td><td>

New

-   FSM AI Native features now support GEMA model as ServiceNow-hosted option. All FSM AI skills and agents have been updated to support the GEMA model, replacing deprecated April and GPT OSS models. Guardian moderation integrations have been validated for compatibility.
-   FSM AI feature gating now supports mixed-SKU customer entitlements. Customers with Pro Plus or Enterprise Plus for FSM retain access to Prime-level AI features even if they hold lower-tier SKUs for other products. Subscription records have been updated to reflect correct product family mappings.

Changed

-   FSM AI subscription records updated for Pro Plus / Foundation mixed-SKU mapping. Product Family fields have been populated on all relevant subscription records, ensuring accurate entitlement resolution for mixed-SKU customers.
-   FSM AI Native skills migrated from deprecated April and GPT OSS models to GEMA. All skills previously targeting April or GPT OSS now default to GEMA or other supported models, ensuring continued functionality.

Removed

-   Sidebar Summarization \(FSM\) asset subscription record has been removed. The asset subscription record for Sidebar Summarization is no longer available, and associated variable sets are not gated behind FSM Advanced.
-   Now LLM LTS Generic provider configs for Work Order Task Summarization have been deprecated. All dormant configurations referencing deprecated LTS models have been deactivated, with no functional gap introduced.

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 for Engagement skills \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. \* The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.Removed\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM Autonomous Workforce

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

-   Service Desk Managers get a Rate trend chart on the AI Specialist Performance dashboard plotting the durable auto-resolve and coverage rates over time, with each day's incidents a click away.
-   Service Desk Managers get a four-step funnel on the Overview tab, from incidents assigned to eligible groups to incidents durably resolved, with drill-down and cards explaining each rate.
-   Service Desk Managers get a chart of what the service desk did with cross-group reassignment recommendations: adopted, overridden, or awaiting a decision.
-   AI Admins can set the portal that knowledge and catalog links open for requesters, while Service Desk Agents keep platform links in work notes.
-   Requesters see the catalog item variables the AI Specialist submitted in the Solution Proposed comment.

Changed

-   Requesters are asked fewer catalog questions, because the AI Specialist fills variables from the incident and the caller's details and shows what it already captured.
-   Requesters receive catalog follow-up questions composed from instruction-mode templates, with the fixed template as a fallback.
-   Service Desk Agents see a knowledge article in Attached Knowledge only once the incident it resolved is closed.
-   Service Desk Managers can apply the Assignment Group and AI Specialist filters together on the All incident outcomes chart.
-   Service Desk Managers on non-English instances see the Catalog tab, funnel, cross-group outcomes, and catalog indicators in their own language, in up to 24 locales.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.
-   Cited knowledge articles were dropped from requester comments by a faulty access check, and were missing from work notes after a low-confidence fallback or reassignment.
-   Saving activity notes failed when research included an uncited result from a table the AI Specialist cannot read.
-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.
-   Resolution notes were cut off at the Helpful References section when none of its links remained.
-   A knowledge feedback task was created for incidents resolved with a catalog item.
-   Incidents stayed In Progress when the Solution proposed state was mapped to Resolved or Closed.
-   A requester comment arriving just after work started could start a duplicate run on the same incident.
-   The closing note after a requester confirmed the fix was always written in English.
-   Canadian French was treated as French.
-   Notes could post with template slots unfilled. The AI Specialist now retries and hands off to a person after three failures.
-   The AI Specialist could ignore the operating system of the computer linked to the incident.
-   Reloading a dashboard drilldown page failed, and unused article links opened a not found page.
-   After an upgrade to version 1.2.2, the dashboard could be missing earlier improvements and show identical first two routing journey metrics.
-   Attachment summarization did not work out of the box, because its service URL property shipped empty.
-   A deprecated AI agent could run instead of the AI Specialist agent after an upgrade.

Removed

-   The Routing journey count cards are gone from the Detailed view, replaced by the Overview funnel.
-   The unused Research depth setting is gone.
-   The Pre resolution condition setting is gone, and incidents are no longer checked against it.

Changed

ServiceNow Otto for IT Service Management \(ITSM\)

</td><td>

18.1.0

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 model updates \* Incident summarization \(New, In progress, Resolved, and Closed\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Record resolution \(resolution notes generation\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Request, requested item, and catalog task summarization now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* Change summarization and change risk explanation now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* The Change risk assessment Assessment Answers Generator Gemini configuration now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash instead of Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. \* Release notes generation now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous configurations are kept but are no longer the default. \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.\* Resolve modal in Service Operations Workspace \* Hyperlinks pasted into Additional comments when resolving an incident are now kept as clickable links.\* AI Search Assist for incident record producers \* AI Search Assist now returns a no-response result when it produces no answer, so the AI Search fallback results are used. \* It now uses the search profile configured for the record producer when one is set.\* Translation fixes addedRemoved\* None.

The Target Generation skill now supports fiscal calendar periods. Generated targets include only the check-in frequencies supported by the active calendar. Unsupported or empty values are replaced with the calendar's default frequency.

Target forms, AI target generation, Goal Insights, actual value automation, and check-in history now support fiscal calendars:

-   Target forms offer only the check-in frequencies that are valid for the active calendar, with defaults based on the calendar type. The available frequencies update automatically when target dates change.
-   Goal Insights and actual value automation correctly handle fiscal period history and boundaries, including time zone edges.
-   Check-in history records use generated fiscal periods, and imports accept Period as a check-in frequency.

Improved the performance and responsiveness of AI status report generation in Project Workspace.

ServiceNow Otto for Virtual Agent

</td><td>

24.0.4

</td><td>

New

-   Admins can now configure advanced voice assistant behaviors through a unified user interface. Admins can enable or disable user interruption management, utterance labeling, transfer deflection, conversation cache, threat detection, dynamic voice pacing, agent matching, and live transfer safeguards directly in the admin interface. Each feature includes editable prompts and settings for granular control.
-   Admins can now enhance the Language and Voice admin page with improved layout and controls. The updated page covers all relevant states, supports auto-populated welcome messages when a secondary language exists, and exposes a dropdown for adding secondary languages. The "What is a welcome message?" section is now presented as an accordion.
-   Admins can now adjust bot pacing \(TTS response speed\) in advanced settings. The response speed slider allows selection from slower to faster, with values stored for each assistant.
-   Admins can now link to UI16 condition setup for Promoted Items from the Assistant edit flow. A direct link enables admins to add conditions for Promoted Items, automatically adjusting their order based on defined criteria.
-   Admins can now pull persona customization data for audit purposes. Instance analyzer exports assistant persona and default persona values for all customer instances in a structured format.
-   Admins can now enable voice mode in Chat UI across all assistant deployments. A new configuration option allows the voice mode button to be shown for all assistants.
-   Admins can now test DTMF input in the Testing UI. During active voice mode test calls, a keypad icon opens a popup with a standard 12-key layout. Keypad input is sent as tones and displayed in the test transcript, supporting both single and multi-language scenarios.
-   Admins can now add omni agents alongside voice agents in the add voice agents modal and landing page. The interface supports adding both agent types for expanded coverage.

Changed

-   Admins experience improved user interface for Language and Voice page configuration. The welcome message auto-populates in English when a secondary language is present, the opening message and language input selection sections are updated, and the "+Add language" button is replaced by a dropdown.
-   Testing UI transcript bubbles have updated styling. Bubble padding is reduced, bubble size is wider, colors match the latest requester experience, and timestamp size is decreased for improved readability.
-   Testing UI now alerts when no AI agents are linked and moves the "Switch to voice mode" banner to a tooltip. The interface provides clearer feedback and guidance during testing.
-   Admin Test UI now sends authentication tokens via request headers. This change improves authentication reliability during testing.

Fixed

-   Positive feedback header for Virtual Agent now matches the preview shown in Assistant Designer.
-   Conversation history is now correctly flushed before applying sensitivity filters, ensuring accurate filtering and transcript management.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for Virtual Agent Configurations

</td><td>

19.0.4

</td><td>

Fixed

Defect fixes.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for Voice Agents

</td><td>

6.2.4

</td><td>

New functionality

-   Conversation safeguards: You can now turn on threat detection on the Safeguards step. A live transfer safeguard, on by default, provides a fallback when a live agent transfer fails.
-   Interruption handling and utterance labeling: Admins can enable context retention when callers interrupt the assistant. Utterance labeling is on by default to reduce false positives. Both features use default prompts that you can edit.
-   Transfer deflection: Admins can set a deflection count for live agent transfers. They can also define instructions for when the count is reached and when it isn't.
-   Custom live agent transfer message: Admins can set the transfer message at the assistant deployment level. If no message is set, the default message is used.
-   Voice pacing: Pacing settings are now stored in the Platform AI Agents app and exposed through the agents API. The new speechRate setting replaces speed\_ratio and gives finer control. Its range is -1.0 to 1.0, and the default is 0.
-   Configurable step-up retry count: Admins can set how many attempts a caller gets at a step-up challenge before the call escalates to a live agent.
-   Omni agent enablement: Agent instructions now pull modality and base instructions from the AI Agent versioning tables.
-   Agent matching performance: The /agents and /agents\_authorized APIs now batch lookups and remove redundant queries, which reduces latency.
-   Testing UI flag: A new analytics flag identifies calls that originated from the Testing UI.

Fixed issues

-   Voice interactions now close explicitly after conversation storage. Previously, they stayed in Work in Progress for about two hours, until the platform idle timeout.
-   Conversations now close correctly after a live agent transfer. Interactions are no longer recorded as closed\_abandoned.
-   The Agents for User Authentication API now returns a structured error instead of an unhandled 500 error when the identification configuration is missing.

Intelligent Approvals

New

Added support for the latest model \(3.5\) of Google Gemini.

</td></tr><tr><td>

IT Service Management AI agent collection

</td><td>

12.1.0

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 for Engagement skills \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. \* The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.Removed\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM - Advanced

</td><td>

2.4.3

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 model updates \* Incident summarization \(New, In progress, Resolved, and Closed\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Record resolution \(resolution notes generation\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Request, requested item, and catalog task summarization now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* Change summarization and change risk explanation now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* The Change risk assessment Assessment Answers Generator Gemini configuration now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash instead of Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. \* Release notes generation now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous configurations are kept but are no longer the default. \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.\* Resolve modal in Service Operations Workspace \* Hyperlinks pasted into Additional comments when resolving an incident are now kept as clickable links.\* AI Search Assist for incident record producers \* AI Search Assist now returns a no-response result when it produces no answer, so the AI Search fallback results are used. \* It now uses the search profile configured for the record producer when one is set.\* Translation fixes addedRemoved\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM - Foundation

</td><td>

2.4.3

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 model updates \* Incident summarization \(New, In progress, Resolved, and Closed\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Record resolution \(resolution notes generation\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Request, requested item, and catalog task summarization now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* Change summarization and change risk explanation now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* The Change risk assessment Assessment Answers Generator Gemini configuration now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash instead of Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. \* Release notes generation now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous configurations are kept but are no longer the default. \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.\* Resolve modal in Service Operations Workspace \* Hyperlinks pasted into Additional comments when resolving an incident are now kept as clickable links.\* AI Search Assist for incident record producers \* AI Search Assist now returns a no-response result when it produces no answer, so the AI Search fallback results are used. \* It now uses the search profile configured for the record producer when one is set.\* Translation fixes addedRemoved\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM Autonomous Workforce

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

-   Service Desk Managers get a Rate trend chart on the AI Specialist Performance dashboard plotting the durable auto-resolve and coverage rates over time, with each day's incidents a click away.
-   Service Desk Managers get a four-step funnel on the Overview tab, from incidents assigned to eligible groups to incidents durably resolved, with drill-down and cards explaining each rate.
-   Service Desk Managers get a chart of what the service desk did with cross-group reassignment recommendations: adopted, overridden, or awaiting a decision.
-   AI Admins can set the portal that knowledge and catalog links open for requesters, while Service Desk Agents keep platform links in work notes.
-   Requesters see the catalog item variables the AI Specialist submitted in the Solution Proposed comment.

Changed

-   Requesters are asked fewer catalog questions, because the AI Specialist fills variables from the incident and the caller's details and shows what it already captured.
-   Requesters receive catalog follow-up questions composed from instruction-mode templates, with the fixed template as a fallback.
-   Service Desk Agents see a knowledge article in Attached Knowledge only once the incident it resolved is closed.
-   Service Desk Managers can apply the Assignment Group and AI Specialist filters together on the All incident outcomes chart.
-   Service Desk Managers on non-English instances see the Catalog tab, funnel, cross-group outcomes, and catalog indicators in their own language, in up to 24 locales.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.
-   Cited knowledge articles were dropped from requester comments by a faulty access check, and were missing from work notes after a low-confidence fallback or reassignment.
-   Saving activity notes failed when research included an uncited result from a table the AI Specialist cannot read.
-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.
-   Resolution notes were cut off at the Helpful References section when none of its links remained.
-   A knowledge feedback task was created for incidents resolved with a catalog item.
-   Incidents stayed In Progress when the Solution proposed state was mapped to Resolved or Closed.
-   A requester comment arriving just after work started could start a duplicate run on the same incident.
-   The closing note after a requester confirmed the fix was always written in English.
-   Canadian French was treated as French.
-   Notes could post with template slots unfilled. The AI Specialist now retries and hands off to a person after three failures.
-   The AI Specialist could ignore the operating system of the computer linked to the incident.
-   Reloading a dashboard drilldown page failed, and unused article links opened a not found page.
-   After an upgrade to version 1.2.2, the dashboard could be missing earlier improvements and show identical first two routing journey metrics.
-   Attachment summarization did not work out of the box, because its service URL property shipped empty.
-   A deprecated AI agent could run instead of the AI Specialist agent after an upgrade.

Removed

-   The Routing journey count cards are gone from the Detailed view, replaced by the Overview funnel.
-   The unused Research depth setting is gone.
-   The Pre resolution condition setting is gone, and incidents are no longer checked against it.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM L1 AI Specialist

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

-   AI Admins can set the portal that knowledge and catalog links open for requesters, while Service Desk Agents keep platform links in work notes.

Changed

-   Requesters receive catalog follow-up questions composed from instruction-mode templates, with the fixed template as a fallback.
-   Service Desk Agents see a knowledge article in Attached Knowledge only once the incident it resolved is closed.
-   Service Desk Managers can apply the Assignment Group and AI Specialist filters together on the All incident outcomes chart.
-   Service Desk Managers on non-English instances see the Catalog tab, funnel, cross-group outcomes, and catalog indicators in their own language, in up to 24 locales.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.

-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.

-   A knowledge feedback task was created for incidents resolved with a catalog item.
-   Incidents stayed In Progress when the Solution proposed state was mapped to Resolved or Closed.
-   A requester comment arriving just after work started could start a duplicate run on the same incident.
-   The closing note after a requester confirmed the fix was always written in English.

-   Notes could post with template slots unfilled. The AI Specialist now retries and hands off to a person after three failures.
-   The AI Specialist could ignore the operating system of the computer linked to the incident.

Field Service Management AI agent collection

</td><td>

4.2.1

</td><td>

New

-   FSM AI Native features now support GEMA model as ServiceNow-hosted option. All FSM AI skills and agents have been updated to support the GEMA model, replacing deprecated April and GPT OSS models. Guardian moderation integrations have been validated for compatibility.
-   FSM AI feature gating now supports mixed-SKU customer entitlements. Customers with Pro Plus or Enterprise Plus for FSM retain access to Prime-level AI features even if they hold lower-tier SKUs for other products. Subscription records have been updated to reflect correct product family mappings.

Changed

-   FSM AI subscription records updated for Pro Plus / Foundation mixed-SKU mapping. Product Family fields have been populated on all relevant subscription records, ensuring accurate entitlement resolution for mixed-SKU customers.
-   FSM AI Native skills migrated from deprecated April and GPT OSS models to GEMA. All skills previously targeting April or GPT OSS now default to GEMA or other supported models, ensuring continued functionality.

Removed

-   Sidebar Summarization \(FSM\) asset subscription record has been removed. The asset subscription record for Sidebar Summarization is no longer available, and associated variable sets are not gated behind FSM Advanced.
-   Now LLM LTS Generic provider configs for Work Order Task Summarization have been deprecated. All dormant configurations referencing deprecated LTS models have been deactivated, with no functional gap introduced.

IT Service Management AI agent collection

</td><td>

12.1.0

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 for Engagement skills \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. \* The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.Removed\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM - Advanced

</td><td>

2.4.3

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 model updates \* Incident summarization \(New, In progress, Resolved, and Closed\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Record resolution \(resolution notes generation\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Request, requested item, and catalog task summarization now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* Change summarization and change risk explanation now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* The Change risk assessment Assessment Answers Generator Gemini configuration now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash instead of Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. \* Release notes generation now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous configurations are kept but are no longer the default. \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.\* Resolve modal in Service Operations Workspace \* Hyperlinks pasted into Additional comments when resolving an incident are now kept as clickable links.\* AI Search Assist for incident record producers \* AI Search Assist now returns a no-response result when it produces no answer, so the AI Search fallback results are used. \* It now uses the search profile configured for the record producer when one is set.\* Translation fixes addedRemoved\* None.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ITSM Autonomous Workforce

</td><td>

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

-   Service Desk Managers get a Rate trend chart on the AI Specialist Performance dashboard plotting the durable auto-resolve and coverage rates over time, with each day's incidents a click away.
-   Service Desk Managers get a four-step funnel on the Overview tab, from incidents assigned to eligible groups to incidents durably resolved, with drill-down and cards explaining each rate.
-   Service Desk Managers get a chart of what the service desk did with cross-group reassignment recommendations: adopted, overridden, or awaiting a decision.
-   AI Admins can set the portal that knowledge and catalog links open for requesters, while Service Desk Agents keep platform links in work notes.
-   Requesters see the catalog item variables the AI Specialist submitted in the Solution Proposed comment.

Changed

-   Requesters are asked fewer catalog questions, because the AI Specialist fills variables from the incident and the caller's details and shows what it already captured.
-   Requesters receive catalog follow-up questions composed from instruction-mode templates, with the fixed template as a fallback.
-   Service Desk Agents see a knowledge article in Attached Knowledge only once the incident it resolved is closed.
-   Service Desk Managers can apply the Assignment Group and AI Specialist filters together on the All incident outcomes chart.
-   Service Desk Managers on non-English instances see the Catalog tab, funnel, cross-group outcomes, and catalog indicators in their own language, in up to 24 locales.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.
-   Cited knowledge articles were dropped from requester comments by a faulty access check, and were missing from work notes after a low-confidence fallback or reassignment.
-   Saving activity notes failed when research included an uncited result from a table the AI Specialist cannot read.
-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.
-   Resolution notes were cut off at the Helpful References section when none of its links remained.
-   A knowledge feedback task was created for incidents resolved with a catalog item.
-   Incidents stayed In Progress when the Solution proposed state was mapped to Resolved or Closed.
-   A requester comment arriving just after work started could start a duplicate run on the same incident.
-   The closing note after a requester confirmed the fix was always written in English.
-   Canadian French was treated as French.
-   Notes could post with template slots unfilled. The AI Specialist now retries and hands off to a person after three failures.
-   The AI Specialist could ignore the operating system of the computer linked to the incident.
-   Reloading a dashboard drilldown page failed, and unused article links opened a not found page.
-   After an upgrade to version 1.2.2, the dashboard could be missing earlier improvements and show identical first two routing journey metrics.
-   Attachment summarization did not work out of the box, because its service URL property shipped empty.
-   A deprecated AI agent could run instead of the AI Specialist agent after an upgrade.

Removed

-   The Routing journey count cards are gone from the Detailed view, replaced by the Overview funnel.
-   The unused Research depth setting is gone.
-   The Pre resolution condition setting is gone, and incidents are no longer checked against it.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.

ServiceNow Otto for Field Service Management

</td><td>

11.2.1

</td><td>

New

-   FSM AI Native features now support GEMA model as ServiceNow-hosted option. All FSM AI skills and agents have been updated to support the GEMA model, replacing deprecated April and GPT OSS models. Guardian moderation integrations have been validated for compatibility.
-   FSM AI feature gating now supports mixed-SKU customer entitlements. Customers with Pro Plus or Enterprise Plus for FSM retain access to Prime-level AI features even if they hold lower-tier SKUs for other products. Subscription records have been updated to reflect correct product family mappings.

Changed

-   FSM AI subscription records updated for Pro Plus / Foundation mixed-SKU mapping. Product Family fields have been populated on all relevant subscription records, ensuring accurate entitlement resolution for mixed-SKU customers.
-   FSM AI Native skills migrated from deprecated April and GPT OSS models to GEMA. All skills previously targeting April or GPT OSS now default to GEMA or other supported models, ensuring continued functionality.

Removed

-   Sidebar Summarization \(FSM\) asset subscription record has been removed. The asset subscription record for Sidebar Summarization is no longer available, and associated variable sets are not gated behind FSM Advanced.
-   Now LLM LTS Generic provider configs for Work Order Task Summarization have been deprecated. All dormant configurations referencing deprecated LTS models have been deactivated, with no functional gap introduced.

</td><td>

6.1.1

</td><td>

New

Premium chat is the enhanced Otto chat experience that coordinates AI agents and skills behind the scenes to complete multi-step tasks.

-   ServiceNow Otto for Setup now runs exclusively on Premium chat. Hierarchical Agents have been disabled, along with the related Hierarchical Agents infrastructure and conversion artifacts.
-   ServiceNow Otto for Setup requires Premium chat. Customers who don't have Premium chat enabled see a banner prompting them to turn it on, with a Go to settings button that links directly to the settings page.

## Suite version 30.2.20261105 - Brazil Patch 2

<table><thead><tr><th>

App name

</th><th>

Version number

</th><th>

Release notes

</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>

@servicenow/sn-ai-engagement-experience

</td><td>

3.8.0

</td><td>

New

New LUX in-product experience components to support inline variant with improved visual designs.

Fixed

-   Resolved auto-refresh issues for sn-agentic-status-card.
-   Resolved issue with AI specialist test component showing an endlessly loading spinner.
-   Rendering issues with LUX components.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Admin Center

</td><td>

6.2.5

</td><td>

New features.

-   First release of AI Admin Center using the next generation Lux application experience and design system.
-   Instance health creates a consolidated view of AI plug-in installation and health tracking with integration back into Application Manager.
-   Upgrade planning analyzes your current instance against a targeted upgrade version allowing you to see what may be changed or impacted during the updgrade process. It covers areas such as new and deprecated features, impacts to customizations, and application compatibility changes all from one place.
-   Intent management enables creation and association of your AI agents to intents for better traceability, reporting, and case management.
-   System property registry provides an admin-facing view into AI related system properties, their descriptions, and current value. It enables transparency and better self-discovery of current config settings impacting AI on the instance.
-   Assist consumption analytics allows you to track and manage your AI usage. It supports views by asset type, usage bands, and user-attributed department.
-   Self-service troubleshooting conversational experience, including issue classification, diagnosis, and recommended remediation for core AI admin use cases.

Other.

-   Minor enhancements to existing capabilities related to AI Agent Advisor and settings.
-   Minor defect fixes.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Admin Hub

</td><td>

11.1.3

</td><td>

 

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Agent Advisor

</td><td>

1.5.1

</td><td>

Fixed

Security fixes.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Agents for AIOps

</td><td>

1.16.1

</td><td>

Fixed

-   Fix REST endpoint ai\_insights/trigger when working in autonomous mode.
-   Cmn\_department record installed by AI Agents for AIOps plugin should be removed.
-   Avoid SRE execution when alert is closed by the AI Specialist.
-   Fix parsing issues in KB skill responses as JSON.
-   During manual assignment to ai worker, unneeded check for 'handle unassigned' is happening.
-   Workflow status marked as error in em\_gai and AI insights lost when autonomous summarization JSON has invalid escape sequences.
-   Run Service Analysis fails on alert\_id input causing 'No record found' error \(4% rate on customer instance\).

Changed

Support Gemma model for AI Search.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Agents for CSM - Complaint Case

</td><td>

1.5.2

</td><td>

Fixed: Defect fix to address the cross scope issues.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Agents for CSM - Major Issue Management

</td><td>

1.0.0

</td><td>

New: Introduces AI-driven similarity search and case correlation to automatically identify related cases, associate them with major cases, or propose new major case candidates.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Agents for Enterprise

</td><td>

1.1.1

</td><td>

Generic LLM model prefixes have been replaced with specific model names for improved clarity and performance: Gemini Flash 3.5 \(formerly gemini\_small\), Claude Haiku 4.5 \(formerly claude\_small\), and GPT 5.4 mini \(formerly gpt\_small\).

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI agents for SLO

</td><td>

2.4.0

</td><td>

Fixed

-   Fixed 400 errors in the ITOM MCP Server CMDB tools.
-   Fixed an issue where the Reliability domain AI agent didn't always return results from the SLO gap AI agent.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Analytics

</td><td>

5.5.2

</td><td>

Fixed

Defects.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Control Tower for Enterprise AI Foundation

</td><td>

1.9.7

</td><td>

Inventory and Discovery.

New

-   Shadow AI: Details on detected AI services, guidance for unknown users and devices, end-user input, an option to hide the UI, and a department block.
-   Connectors: Trace Collector discovers Tools. Hyperscaler connectors install automatically. Anthropic discovers Agents via Beta APIs and supports MID servers. GCP imports Gemini Enterprise App Agents with clearer errors. Microsoft adds attributes and tag-based discovery. Databricks adds Domain Separation. GitHub discovers organizations, repositories, and Copilot agents, with scheduled imports and Cloud and Enterprise support.

Changed

-   Shadow AI lifetime aggregations limited to 3 years.
-   GCP Beta API usage UX updated.

Fixed

-   Shadow AI UI performance and registry filtering.
-   AI Discovery Agentic Client support and staging domain fallback.
-   Anthropic false success on Test Connection; LangGraph duplicates and standalone run usage; several GCP issues.

Govern.

New

-   AI and Data Marketplace: Business users choose from approved use cases and models.
-   AI Policy Framework v1.2: MVP release with kill switch.

Fixed

-   Playbook and lifecycle fixes in AI Risk and Compliance, and AI Risk and Asset Management.
-   Rejection comments display; rejected requests set to "Cancelled."

Secure.

New

-   AI Security Operator role: Deactivate and reinstate agents without full AI Steward access.
-   AWH for AI Control Tower: Client-Based Access Control \(CBAC\).

Changed

-   Top recommendations ranks agents by critical security events.
-   Action required on upgrade: Base Traceloop Warehouse connection removed. Recreate it and its credential if you use Traceloop-based metrics. Sampling and max-skill-call defaults reset.
-   Security tab split into Overview, Runtime, Post-runtime, and Security insights.
-   Renames: "Correctness detection" and "Agent containment list."
-   Consent required for Post-runtime and Security insights settings.

Fixed

-   Four customer-reported bugs, including silent AWS Bedrock block/unblock failures.
-   Multiple fixes across anonymization, heatmaps, agent maps, Overview load time, and CBAC policy names.

Monitor.

New

-   Direct trace access for usage mismatches.
-   Severity-based grouping of score drops.
-   Agent activity chart filters by top or bottom five metrics.
-   Metric scores and LLM judge reasoning on session details.
-   Input and output toggle in trace details.

Changed

-   Arc traces evaluated at asset level for Agentic Client assets; Tools discovery and evaluation added.
-   Reduced insight noise; widget customization across AICT pages.

Fixed

-   Redundant session scores removed; Privileged Access Detector shows 0% in red.
-   Custom date selection and duplicate insights.

Measure.

Fixed

-   Functional and non-functional IT defects.
-   Creator metrics for managed and unmanaged AI systems.

Foundations.

Changed

AINPX UI: AI Gateway access management, runtime security configuration, a Security Insights tab, connector upgrades, and LLM-based deduplication.

Fixed

-   EA for AICT domain separation on AI Portfolio widgets.
-   AWH: correct Tool Groups count, MCP sync job errors, and catalog population on new instances.
-   Otto: manufacturer, vendor, and owned\_by details resolve correctly.

Full details: https://www.servicenow.com/docs/r/release-notes/ai-control-tower-rn.html.

</td></tr><tr><td>

AI Native Experience for AI Control Tower - ServicenowAI

</td><td>

4.0.6

</td><td>

AI Control Tower adds new capabilities across discovery, governance, security, monitoring, and measurement of AI usage.

Inventory and Discovery

Shadow AI Discovery

New

-   Details about the AI service detected, including models used, users, departments, devices, and event data.
-   Guidance on unknown users/devices.
-   End-user input surfaced for AI services detected via ACC.
-   Ability to turn off this UI for data protection.
-   Block capability for departments using an AI service detected via ACC.

Changed

Lifetime aggregations are limited to 3 years.

Govern

New

AI and Data Marketplace : Streamline the request process for business users by allowing them to select from already approved use cases and models, reducing friction and complexity in submitting requests.

Secure

New

AI Security Operator role - lets teams delegate agent containment \(deactivate/reinstate\) without granting full AI Steward access.

Changed

-   Smarter Overview recommendations - "Top Recommendations" now ranks agents by critical security events \(with agent name, event count, threat categories\) instead of mixing in the raw events list; Overview and Post-runtime event lists are now consistent with each other.
-   Four customer-reported bugs Fixed: stale tabs after navigation, "View source record" misrouting to raw trace data, a hidden Security and Privacy tab in the legacy UI, and silent AWS Bedrock block/unblock failures.
-   Action required on upgrade: the base Traceloop Warehouse connection is Removed: customers using Traceloop-based external AI metrics must recreate the connection and credential; sampling-rate/max-skill-call defaults also reset \(existing Detection-enabled settings are untouched\).

Monitor

Changed

-   Arc dependency - Discover and Evaluate Arc traces at Asset Level for assets categorized as 'Agentic Client'.
-   Tools discovery and evaluation.
-   UI design enhancements for metric line graph and session details page.
-   Arc-Mosaic dependency to support InstanceInfoAPI.
-   TL insights enhancements \(Group insights to reduce the noise\).
-   Update EvalPurposeUsage\(Mismatch\) insight to redirect to trace-level view.
-   Add aggregated metric insight \(with categorize data\) with severity-level grouping.
-   Widget customization capability support across ALL AICT pages.

Measure

New

-   Sub-vendor AI costs: Set a rate for a specific service from an integrated vendor, such as an agent platform. The sub-vendor rate takes precedence over the vendor rate. The vendor rate applies to any usage that doesn't have a sub-vendor rate.
-   Subscription AI costs: Record per-seat subscription costs for integrated and non-integrated vendors. Specify the number of licensed seats, the cost per seat, and the contract dates. Optionally, add a one-time setup fee and distribute it over a set number of months so that it's included in the estimated monthly cost.

Changed

Assign costs to specific services from integrated vendors.

Include per-seat subscription costs in AI cost tracking.

For full details, see AI Control Tower release notes.

Customer Service Management AI agent collection

</td><td>

9.0.2

</td><td>

Similar historical case search: Fixed the similar historical case search for the CRM AI Specialist, so it finds and returns similar past cases correctly.

Duplicate detection: Enhanced duplicate detection for the CRM AI Specialist.

-   A confirmed duplicate now uses a single duplicate\_detected outcome across the worker state mapping, response template choices, CRM Case AI Agent instructions and the Post Activity Note action. The case closes and the run finishes instead of ending in Awaiting Info.
-   Duplicate detection now runs before sentiment in assess\_case. A duplicate skips sentiment, intent and field prediction. An escalation skips only field prediction.
-   Duplicates are handled in both execution modes. Autopilot posts the customer note and closes the case. Copilot keeps the case closed instead of routing it back to New and reassigning it.
-   The agent no longer waits for a customer reply after a duplicate note, so it doesn't run the follow-up step on a closed case.
-   Dashboard analytics record duplicate\_detected as the closed state.
-   The fetch\_activity\_note\_template and Save Activity Notes tools list the exact allowed states \(solution\_proposed, awaiting\_info, reassign, duplicate\_detected\). The agent now posts the correct customer note for a duplicate instead of the solution-proposed one.

Record lookup and Triage cases wrap-up

-   Records now resolve correctly when record numbers are duplicated across tables.
-   The Triage cases wrap-up can now update work notes by record number.

Triage cases flows: The "Interaction - Triage cases" trigger flows now run as the session user instead of the system user. Actions in these flows now respect the user's access and show the right user as the actor.

New

-   FSM AI Native features now support GEMA model as ServiceNow-hosted option. All FSM AI skills and agents have been updated to support the GEMA model, replacing deprecated April and GPT OSS models. Guardian moderation integrations have been validated for compatibility.
-   FSM AI feature gating now supports mixed-SKU customer entitlements. Customers with Pro Plus or Enterprise Plus for FSM retain access to Prime-level AI features even if they hold lower-tier SKUs for other products. Subscription records have been updated to reflect correct product family mappings.

Changed

-   FSM AI subscription records updated for Pro Plus / Foundation mixed-SKU mapping. Product Family fields have been populated on all relevant subscription records, ensuring accurate entitlement resolution for mixed-SKU customers.
-   FSM AI Native skills migrated from deprecated April and GPT OSS models to GEMA. All skills previously targeting April or GPT OSS now default to GEMA or other supported models, ensuring continued functionality.

Removed

-   Sidebar Summarization \(FSM\) asset subscription record has been removed. The asset subscription record for Sidebar Summarization is no longer available, and associated variable sets are not gated behind FSM Advanced.
-   Now LLM LTS Generic provider configs for Work Order Task Summarization have been deprecated. All dormant configurations referencing deprecated LTS models have been deactivated, with no functional gap introduced.

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 for Engagement skills \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. \* The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.Removed\* None.

1.3.0

</td><td>

New

-   Service Desk Managers get a Rate trend chart on the AI Specialist Performance dashboard plotting the durable auto-resolve and coverage rates over time, with each day's incidents a click away.
-   Service Desk Managers get a four-step funnel on the Overview tab, from incidents assigned to eligible groups to incidents durably resolved, with drill-down and cards explaining each rate.
-   Service Desk Managers get a chart of what the service desk did with cross-group reassignment recommendations: adopted, overridden, or awaiting a decision.
-   AI Admins can set the portal that knowledge and catalog links open for requesters, while Service Desk Agents keep platform links in work notes.
-   Requesters see the catalog item variables the AI Specialist submitted in the Solution Proposed comment.

Changed

-   Requesters are asked fewer catalog questions, because the AI Specialist fills variables from the incident and the caller's details and shows what it already captured.
-   Requesters receive catalog follow-up questions composed from instruction-mode templates, with the fixed template as a fallback.
-   Service Desk Agents see a knowledge article in Attached Knowledge only once the incident it resolved is closed.
-   Service Desk Managers can apply the Assignment Group and AI Specialist filters together on the All incident outcomes chart.
-   Service Desk Managers on non-English instances see the Catalog tab, funnel, cross-group outcomes, and catalog indicators in their own language, in up to 24 locales.

Fixed

-   Access control gaps let users or prompt-injected tool calls read records beyond their permissions through the Catalog Agent and the AI Specialist's tools, and let generative AI steps run without checking the triggering user's access.
-   Cited knowledge articles were dropped from requester comments by a faulty access check, and were missing from work notes after a low-confidence fallback or reassignment.
-   Saving activity notes failed when research included an uncited result from a table the AI Specialist cannot read.
-   Knowledge and catalog links in Helpful References opened the platform view instead of the portal.
-   Resolution notes were cut off at the Helpful References section when none of its links remained.
-   A knowledge feedback task was created for incidents resolved with a catalog item.
-   Incidents stayed In Progress when the Solution proposed state was mapped to Resolved or Closed.
-   A requester comment arriving just after work started could start a duplicate run on the same incident.
-   The closing note after a requester confirmed the fix was always written in English.
-   Canadian French was treated as French.
-   Notes could post with template slots unfilled. The AI Specialist now retries and hands off to a person after three failures.
-   The AI Specialist could ignore the operating system of the computer linked to the incident.
-   Reloading a dashboard drilldown page failed, and unused article links opened a not found page.
-   After an upgrade to version 1.2.2, the dashboard could be missing earlier improvements and show identical first two routing journey metrics.
-   Attachment summarization did not work out of the box, because its service URL property shipped empty.
-   A deprecated AI agent could run instead of the AI Specialist agent after an upgrade.

Removed

-   The Routing journey count cards are gone from the Detailed view, replaced by the Overview funnel.
-   The unused Research depth setting is gone.
-   The Pre resolution condition setting is gone, and incidents are no longer checked against it.

</td></tr><tr><td>
