18.1.0

</td><td>

Changed\* Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 model updates \* Incident summarization \(New, In progress, Resolved, and Closed\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Record resolution \(resolution notes generation\) now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous alias-based configurations are kept at lower priority. \* Request, requested item, and catalog task summarization now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* Change summarization and change risk explanation now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of the previous small-model aliases. \* The Change risk assessment Assessment Answers Generator Gemini configuration now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash instead of Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. \* Release notes generation now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous configurations are kept but are no longer the default. \* The Classify consent and Simplify description skills now use Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 by default. The previous Gemini and Claude configurations for these skills are kept but are now inactive and no longer the default. \* The Engagement Message Composer skill now uses Gemini 3.5 Flash and Claude Sonnet 4.6 instead of its previous Gemini and Claude models.Fixed\* Create incident AI agent in Virtual Agent \* The agent now shows a closing message after the user confirms that the issue is resolved.\* Investigate and resolve incidents \* The Get relevant knowledge articles tool now returns knowledge article results on instances where the knowledge semantic index configuration differs from the default names. Previously it returned "No information found." \* Investigate and recommend incident agents no longer fail with "Error while finding catalog items" when other plugins, such as Intelligent Approval, add more records.\* Find catalog item AI agent \* The agent now generates a response in AI Agent Studio.\* Incident classification and triage \* The Categorize ITSM incident AI agent no longer displays the caller's CI. It now covers category and subcategory only. \* The Get Incident and relevant major incidents and problems tool now uses the single \[RA-ITSM\] AI Search Profile when it retrieves similar incidents.\* Post incident report \* Line breaks in the Post Incident Report sections are now preserved when they are written to the incident Overview field.\* Change outage assistant AI agent \* The agent now converts dates entered in the user's date format to ISO 8601 before passing them to its tools. Previously the Validate planned start and end date and Create outage tools returned errors for users with a date format such as MM-dd-yyyy. \* When a date is ambiguous, the agent now asks the user to confirm the day and month.\* Suggest configuration items for a change request workflow \* The Configuration Item Suggestion no longer shows tags.\* Flow Designer \* The application no longer deletes a global Flow Designer complex object. Previously flows could start failing after the application was installed.\* Resolve modal in Service Operations Workspace \* Hyperlinks pasted into Additional comments when resolving an incident are now kept as clickable links.\* AI Search Assist for incident record producers \* AI Search Assist now returns a no-response result when it produces no answer, so the AI Search fallback results are used. \* It now uses the search profile configured for the record producer when one is set.\* Translation fixes addedRemoved\* None.

The Target Generation skill now supports fiscal calendar periods. Generated targets include only the check-in frequencies supported by the active calendar. Unsupported or empty values are replaced with the calendar's default frequency.

Target forms, AI target generation, Goal Insights, actual value automation, and check-in history now support fiscal calendars:

-   Target forms offer only the check-in frequencies that are valid for the active calendar, with defaults based on the calendar type. The available frequencies update automatically when target dates change.
-   Goal Insights and actual value automation correctly handle fiscal period history and boundaries, including time zone edges.
-   Check-in history records use generated fiscal periods, and imports accept Period as a check-in frequency.

Improved the performance and responsiveness of AI status report generation in Project Workspace.

ServiceNow Otto for Virtual Agent

</td><td>

24.0.4

</td><td>

New

-   Admins can now configure advanced voice assistant behaviors through a unified user interface. Admins can enable or disable user interruption management, utterance labeling, transfer deflection, conversation cache, threat detection, dynamic voice pacing, agent matching, and live transfer safeguards directly in the admin interface. Each feature includes editable prompts and settings for granular control.
-   Admins can now enhance the Language and Voice admin page with improved layout and controls. The updated page covers all relevant states, supports auto-populated welcome messages when a secondary language exists, and exposes a dropdown for adding secondary languages. The "What is a welcome message?" section is now presented as an accordion.
-   Admins can now adjust bot pacing \(TTS response speed\) in advanced settings. The response speed slider allows selection from slower to faster, with values stored for each assistant.
-   Admins can now link to UI16 condition setup for Promoted Items from the Assistant edit flow. A direct link enables admins to add conditions for Promoted Items, automatically adjusting their order based on defined criteria.
-   Admins can now pull persona customization data for audit purposes. Instance analyzer exports assistant persona and default persona values for all customer instances in a structured format.
-   Admins can now enable voice mode in Chat UI across all assistant deployments. A new configuration option allows the voice mode button to be shown for all assistants.
-   Admins can now test DTMF input in the Testing UI. During active voice mode test calls, a keypad icon opens a popup with a standard 12-key layout. Keypad input is sent as tones and displayed in the test transcript, supporting both single and multi-language scenarios.
-   Admins can now add omni agents alongside voice agents in the add voice agents modal and landing page. The interface supports adding both agent types for expanded coverage.

Changed

-   Admins experience improved user interface for Language and Voice page configuration. The welcome message auto-populates in English when a secondary language is present, the opening message and language input selection sections are updated, and the "+Add language" button is replaced by a dropdown.
-   Testing UI transcript bubbles have updated styling. Bubble padding is reduced, bubble size is wider, colors match the latest requester experience, and timestamp size is decreased for improved readability.
-   Testing UI now alerts when no AI agents are linked and moves the "Switch to voice mode" banner to a tooltip. The interface provides clearer feedback and guidance during testing.
-   Admin Test UI now sends authentication tokens via request headers. This change improves authentication reliability during testing.

Fixed

-   Positive feedback header for Virtual Agent now matches the preview shown in Assistant Designer.
-   Conversation history is now correctly flushed before applying sensitivity filters, ensuring accurate filtering and transcript management.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for Virtual Agent Configurations

</td><td>

19.0.4

</td><td>

Fixed

Defect fixes.

</td></tr><tr><td>

ServiceNow Otto for Voice Agents

</td><td>

6.2.4

</td><td>

New functionality

-   Conversation safeguards: You can now turn on threat detection on the Safeguards step. A live transfer safeguard, on by default, provides a fallback when a live agent transfer fails.
-   Interruption handling and utterance labeling: Admins can enable context retention when callers interrupt the assistant. Utterance labeling is on by default to reduce false positives. Both features use default prompts that you can edit.
-   Transfer deflection: Admins can set a deflection count for live agent transfers. They can also define instructions for when the count is reached and when it isn't.
-   Custom live agent transfer message: Admins can set the transfer message at the assistant deployment level. If no message is set, the default message is used.
-   Voice pacing: Pacing settings are now stored in the Platform AI Agents app and exposed through the agents API. The new speechRate setting replaces speed\_ratio and gives finer control. Its range is -1.0 to 1.0, and the default is 0.
-   Configurable step-up retry count: Admins can set how many attempts a caller gets at a step-up challenge before the call escalates to a live agent.
-   Omni agent enablement: Agent instructions now pull modality and base instructions from the AI Agent versioning tables.
-   Agent matching performance: The /agents and /agents\_authorized APIs now batch lookups and remove redundant queries, which reduces latency.
-   Testing UI flag: A new analytics flag identifies calls that originated from the Testing UI.

Fixed issues

-   Voice interactions now close explicitly after conversation storage. Previously, they stayed in Work in Progress for about two hours, until the platform idle timeout.
-   Conversations now close correctly after a live agent transfer. Interactions are no longer recorded as closed\_abandoned.
-   The Agents for User Authentication API now returns a structured error instead of an unhandled 500 error when the identification configuration is missing.
